 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.3.8i</th>
  <td>Test Procedure</td>
  <td>[FOCR 262] Each odor panel member shall be exposed to only one odor specimen in 48 hr.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.3.9a</th>
  <td>Reporting</td>
  <td>[FOCR 263] The test report shall include specimen identification, configuration, test conditions, and the<br/>average rating from the odor panel members.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.3.9b</th>
  <td>Reporting</td>
  <td>[FOCR 264] Any nasal irritation or other abnormal conditions from the medical examination shall be<br/>recorded.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.3.9c</th>
  <td>Reporting</td>
  <td>[FOCR 265] If the material is determined to be unsafe for testing (based on the offgassing analysis from<br/>Test 7), the reasons for the determination not to perform Test 6 on the specimen shall be included.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.3.9d</th>
  <td>Reporting</td>
  <td>[FOCR 266] The test report shall be submitted to MAPTIS in a MAPTIS-compatible format.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.3.10</th>
  <td>Precision</td>
  <td>[FOCR 267] Measurements shall be made to the following precision:<br/>a. Absolute pressure: ±1 percent of reading.<br/>b. Temperature: ±3 °C (±5.4 °F).<br/>c. Oxygen concentration: ±0.5 percent of reading.<br/>d. Mass: ±0.01</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.3.11</th>
  <td>Odor Panel<br/>Member</td>
  <td>[FOCR 268] Each odor panel member shall be requalified every 4 months or before conducting an odor<br/>test, using the procedure described in section A.2.3.7.a in this NASA Technical Standard.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.4.2</th>
  <td>Configurational<br/>Flammability<br/>(Test 10), Test<br/>Criteria</td>
  <td>[FOCR 269] Test 10 shall be performed in accordance with the test procedure described in Test 1.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.4.3a</th>
  <td>Configurational<br/>Flammability<br/>(Test 10), Test<br/>Method</td>
  <td>[FOCR 270] A test plan specific for the worst-case use environment (maximum temperature, pressure,<br/>and oxygen concentration), placement of ignition source, use configuration, and application shall be<br/>developed by the test organization in conjunction with the test requester.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.4.3b</th>
  <td>Configurational<br/>Flammability<br/>(Test 10), Test<br/>Method</td>
  <td>[FOCR 271] The acceptance criteria shall be determined by the responsible NASA program/project M&amp;P<br/>organization.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5 Mechanical</th>
  <td>for</td>
  <td>in Ambient Pressure LOX and Mechanical for Materials in Variable Pressure</td>
  <td>and</td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.2a</th>
  <td>Test Summary</td>
  <td>[FOCR 272] The test specimens shall be 17.5 mm (0.69 in) in diameter.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.2b</th>
  <td>Test Summary</td>
  <td>[FOCR 273] Materials shall be tested in their use thickness for thicknesses up to 6.35 mm (0.25 in).</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.2c</th>

  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
</table>

 <tr>

 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.2d</th>
  <td>Test Summary</td>
  <td>[FOCR 275] The specimens shall be subjected to mechanical impact at the desired energy and inspected<br/>for evidence of a reaction.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.3a</th>
  <td>Test Criteria</td>
  <td>[FOCR 276] When impacted at 98 J (72 ft-lb), 20 specimens shall not react.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.3b</th>
  <td>Test Criteria</td>
  <td>[FOCR 277] If 1 specimen out of 20 reacts, 40 additional specimens shall be tested without any<br/>reactions.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.5a</th>
  <td>Test Conditions</td>
  <td>[FOCR 278] For ambient pressure LOX tests, the test conditions (pressure and temperature) shall be the<br/>test facility ambient pressure and the LOX boiling point at that pressure.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.5b</th>
  <td>Test Conditions</td>
  <td>[FOCR 279] For variable pressure LOX and GOX tests, the test conditions (pressure and temperature)<br/>shall be determined for each test as recommended in the OCA.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.6a</th>
  <td>Specimen<br/>Preparation</td>
  <td>[FOCR 280] Materials shall be prepared as stated in ASTM G86, Standard Test Method for Determining<br/>Ignition Sensitivity of Materials to Mechanical Impact in Ambient Liquid Oxygen and Pressurized<br/>and Gaseous Environments.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.6b</th>
  <td>Specimen<br/>Preparation</td>
  <td>[FOCR 281] The test material dimensions specified in ASTM G86 shall be used, unless otherwise<br/>specified by the responsible NASA program/project M&amp;P organization.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.6c</th>
  <td>Specimen</td>
  <td>[FOCR 282] The specimen shall be inspected and flaws noted.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.6d</th>
  <td>Specimen<br/>Preparation</td>
  <td>[FOCR 283] If the flaws result from specimen preparation at the test facility, new specimens shall be<br/>prepared.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.6e</th>
  <td>Specimen</td>
  <td>[FOCR 284] When testing materials to determine relative ranking, specimen preparation (including<br/>and condition thickness, surface finish, shall be identical.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.7</th>
  <td>Test System</td>
  <td>[FOCR 285] The test system shall be identical to that described in ASTM G86.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.8</th>
  <td>Pre-Test<br/>Procedure</td>
  <td>[FOCR 286] The pre-test procedure, including calibration of the test system, shall be identical to that<br/>described in ASTM G86.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.9</th>
  <td>Test Procedure</td>
  <td>[FOCR 287] The test procedure shall be identical to that described in ASTM G86, with the exception that<br/>is at the and</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.10a</th>
  <td>Reporting</td>
  <td>[FOCR 288] The test report shall include specimen identification, configuration, test conditions, number<br/>of reactions, and observations from the test.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.10b</th>
  <td>Reporting</td>
  <td>[FOCR 289] The test report shall be submitted to MAPTIS in a MAPTIS-compatible format.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.5.11</th>
  <td>Precision</td>
  <td>[FOCR 290] Precision of measurements (for example, specimen dimensions, drop height, and time) shall<br/>be identical to ASTM G86.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th></th>
  <td></td>
  <td>A.2.6 Gaseous Fluid Impact for Components</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>A.2.6.2a</th>
  <td>Test Summary</td>
  <td>[FOCR 291] The test specimens shall be components in their normal use condition, including the end-use<br/>cleanliness level.</td>

## Section Description


NASA-STD-6001B w/CHANGE 2


NASA-STD-6001B w/CHANGE 2


Requirement in this Standard


Specimens are cup-shaped with an outside diameter of 0.99 cm (0.390 in) and a target
surface thickness of 1.5 cm (0.060 in). Each specimen is press fit onto a copper
specimen holder for testing.


Applicable If No,
(Yes or Enter
No) Rationale


Figure 9-High-Velocity Particle Impact Target Specimen Configuration
(all dimensions are in inches)


B. Low-Velocity Particle Impact Material Specimen Preparation (figure 10, Low-Velocity
Particle Impact Target Specimen Configuration):


Specimens are flat discs, 1.5 cm (0.060 in) thick, with holes drilled radially to allow flow
through the outer edge of the target.

 </tr>

  <td></td>
 </tr>

  <td></td>
 </tr>

  <td>Purpose</td>

  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>

  <td>Purpose</td>

  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>B.6</th>
  <td>Purpose</td>
  <td>[FOCR 408] The certification panel shall perform an on-site inspection to ensure that the test facility can<br/>the methods from of a material to issuance of a final</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>B.7</th>
  <td>Purpose</td>
  <td>[FOCR 409] The certification panel shall recommend to the NASA Office of the Chief Engineer<br/>certification of the test on the basis of with this document.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>
  <th>B.8</th>
  <td>Purpose</td>
  <td>[FOCR 410] The certification panel and test facility shall maintain certification records.</td>
  <td></td>
  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>

  <td>Purpose</td>

  <td></td>
 </tr>
 <tr>

  <td>Purpose</td>

 </tr>
